Native reference is clipped from the retained composite without rescaling either source differently. Preview height now closely aligns; avatar frames/badges, native Buy-in range, row occupancy graphics, account services and exact seated controls remain different/unverified. Static changes serve on live reload, no backend restart or account mutation. Generated images approximately 0.9 MiB; no new dependencies.

Native-style cash-list occupancy: retained native selected-table capture shows one small gold/grey block per seat. Standard Hold’em/PLO/Short Deck Table View now renders capacity-sized blocks from actual hosted occupancy; filled gold slots represent occupied seats, grey slots are empty. Unknown occupancy remains an em dash, AoF/group views retain their separately observed formats, and every block group exposes an accessible count plus hover text. Red browser assertion failed on the absent marker; final Hold’em and PLO two-hand integrations pass full/empty markers, six-seat preview, independent payouts, carried balances, 720 chips and restart/release. PLO additionally verifies the already-open lobby updates from six gold blocks to zero after release. Updated 1200×957 local-selected-table.png and equal-frame selected-comparison.png were visually inspected and browser comparison validation passes. Live static assets serve the change on reload without server restart. Block colors/dimensions approximate the retained native capture; full lobby parity, player badges and waiting-list behavior remain incomplete.

Between-hand cash leave/replacement: completed hosted cash hands now offer Leave table. The authenticated seat is revoked, its exact stack is acknowledged as a practice cash-out and recorded privately (latest 100 receipts), and remaining seat stacks persist in a waiting state. No wallet/real-money transfer is implied. A replacement starts the next numbered/dealer-rotated hand with its fresh practice buy-in and the others' retained stacks; existing busted-stack practice rebuys retain their prior policy. Shared cashHand handles initial/fill/ready/replacement consistently. Public summary/felt show retained balances while waiting; old private cards disappear with the finished hand. Active-hand leave and started-tournament leave remain rejected. Engine red test reproduced original blanket leave rejection, then verifies amount/chip conservation, vacancy, token revocation, restart, dealer/number, private replacement cards and atomic rollback on write failure. Six-browser UI test completes unequal second hand, leaves folded seat 3 for an exact 112-chip receipt, observes five retained seats/stacks, joins Replacement into seat 3, then verifies third-hand privacy and exact starting balances. Two-hand five-computer, readiness, tick, AoF and 3/6-seat tournament regressions pass. Applied live only with no active hands; nine hosted public snapshots and all file digests unchanged. Native stand-up placement/behavior, leaving mid-hand, wallet funding and waiting-list automation remain incomplete/unverified.

Short-handed hosted cash play: first seated host can Start hand with at least two participants without filling all six (four in AoF) positions. Empty slots receive zero chips/cards and no blinds; cashHand chooses an occupied initial dealer and uses engine nextDealer thereafter, including sparse heads-up transitions. Only seated participants count toward next-hand readiness. New cash joins during an active hand reserve their empty seat and wait for the next deal, with no chips/cards/current-pot changes; filling the last vacancy cannot reset the active hand. Their practice stack is funded at the next deal. Reservations show an explicit waiting status. Red unit test failed on missing canStart; final tests cover host/minimum guards, heads-up seats 3/6 and correct blinds, two-to-three transition, empty-card privacy, restart, all PLO variants/Short Deck/AoF, and all remaining vacancies reserved mid-PLO6 hand without a reset. Browser --short-handed creates via admin, joins isolated seats 3/6, starts with two, reserves seat 1 privately, plays a full heads-up showdown with independent payout/240 chips, readies three participants, verifies 360-chip next deal/blinds and server recovery. Full six-browser unequal hand/uncalled returns/leave/replacement regression still passes, as do readiness/timer/tournament checks. Live activation with no active hands preserves all files and nine hosted public snapshots. Host Start control and funding/rebuy policy are local behavior, not an observed native seated-control match; native automatic-deal timing and pending-player presentation remain unverified.

Native WebKit audio gate: native QA now instruments actual AudioBufferSourceNode.start calls and refuses a completed hand unless at least one decoded clip with positive duration was started through the real WebKit API. Full visible AppKit run started 35 sources and still passed six-player independent payout, 720 chips and cold relaunch. Optional PLAYGG_AUDIO_PROBE=1 also uses the existing ScreenCaptureKit meter scoped to that exact ephemeral QA app bundle, saves no samples, and completes remaining relaunch/payout checks before returning its audio-gate failure. Observed 259200 frames with peak 0.0; retained metadata is home-art/rea/local-webkit-audio-meter.json. This is unverified/silent attribution, not proof of audible output or success. WebKit helper-process attribution is a hypothesis only. Source-start calls prove the software path, not the final device signal. Default original-client meter targeting remains available without an explicit bundle argument. No production audio code or live account/table changed. Native speaker playback, signal attribution and original timing remain open.

Flicker repair (user report): betting actions rebuilt every felt seat/card, and live cash-catalog updates rebuilt all rows/preview artwork. Added a small shared element updater in existing felt.mjs that retains matching mounted elements, changes only differing attributes/text/children and refreshes click handlers. Turn clocks use a deadline render key so an unchanged active clock/animation survives unrelated updates; fresh unused animations are cancelled. Network now retains seats, portraits, hole cards, board/pot nodes and reaction images, and updates payout stacks on the live mounted node with a hand-number guard. The active betting panel stays mounted for same-hand/turn/history updates, preserving prepared amounts and confirmation dialogs when a player joins. Cash lobby rows, headings and selected-table artwork update in place. Hidden Home carousel stops rotating behind catalog/profile/settings screens. Browser red assertion reproduced remounted table artwork; final Hold’em and Omaha two-hand tests retain seat/avatar/hole-card and lobby row/avatar/felt object identities through action/stack updates and still pass independent payouts, reveal/WIN/award order, 720 chips and reconnect/release. Short-handed browser test proves late join retains the identical bet input, open confirmation dialog, live clock element and running animation. Native visible WKWebView hand, card/name geometry, payout and cold relaunch also pass. Refreshed the user's existing live PLO lobby in IAB and observed all three real Omaha tables load. No backend restart or live game mutation. This addresses verified remount/carousel causes; every screen/display-specific flicker and complete native parity remain unverified.

Native audio signal follow-up: isolated WKWebView analyser inserted transparently between the existing gain node and destination measured decoded peak 0.88014 and rendered peak 0.79985 over 119 polls with 29 decoded source starts during the post-flicker native acceptance run. Retained local-webkit-audio-signal.json records the exact scope. This proves nonzero signal in the real browser audio graph, while the earlier exact-parent-app ScreenCaptureKit tap remains silent. Helper attribution and physical speakers remain unverified; no claim of native sound timing equivalence follows.

Rush/tournament flicker follow-through: retained focused Rush pool controls and preview across the existing two-second refresh, and retained tournament rows/selected details across selection/data updates using the same shared DOM updater. Missing controls and no-result link cleanup remain unchanged. New red assertions reproduced Rush focus/row remount and tournament details remount before the fix. Final event-filter test verifies mounted row/details plus range validation, reset, category filtering and event entry. Both Hold’em and PLO solo Rush tests now verify row/preview identity and focus after refresh, then complete overlapping hands, independent payouts, 2400 chips and actual backend restart. Four isolated tournament browsers still reach a winner with blind increases, three eliminations, 400 chips and a persisted 100-credit prize. All checks pass. Only playgg.mjs and browser regression coverage changed; live backend/state untouched, static update available on page reload. This extends the known remount fix, not a claim of every possible display/browser flicker being eliminated.

Home carousel decode/swap flicker: visible promotion and edge artwork previously changed src immediately, before replacement images decoded. display now decodes cloned artwork first, commits the loaded pair in the same task, and updates dots only after commit. Request sequence keeps the latest rapid-click intent; outdated results cannot overwrite it, decode failure retains the last complete frame, and auto-advance waits while a request is loading. Previous/Next use requested intent so rapid clicks do not collapse into one step. Browser red test observed premature visible src changes before implementation. Final --carousel test delays real decode completion, verifies old frame retained, resolves an obsolete request, commits only the latest matching pair, checks complete/nonzero-size images, injects failure and verifies previous-control recovery. Existing local login/seat/logout and full settings/geometry/live-sync tests pass. Refreshed the existing live PLO IAB tab only after observing no open overlay or focused form control, then verified actual Omaha rows loaded. No backend restart, dependencies or live table/account mutation. Exact native carousel fade/timing and display-specific flicker remain unverified.

Late-entry cancellation: undealt cash reservations now offer Cancel waiting seat while a hand runs. The seat is removed and its credential revoked without changing the hand, deck, cards, turn, committed chips, deadline, stacks or cash-out ledger. Dealt players and started tournaments retain their leave restrictions. The waiting action uses a stable render key across other players' actions and remains a single mounted button. Engine red test reproduced the original blanket rejection; final sparse engine test verifies exact unchanged hand, occupancy drop, old-token rejection, persistence and re-reservation before funding next hand. Isolated browser flow cancels/rejoins through controls, observes the real seat removal/return, preserves prepared actions and clock, checks waiting-button identity/count during play, independently verifies heads-up payout and next-hand three-player funding/blinds/restart. Cash-leave, readiness, deadline and 3/6-player tournament checks pass. Applied live while idle; nine public table snapshots and all saved-file hashes unchanged. Native cancellation wording/placement and dealt-player mid-hand stand-up remain unverified/incomplete.


Owner-controlled local backups: authenticated local-only /api/owner-backups supports listing/creating private snapshots and verifying one constrained ID. Snapshots preserve exact checkpoint bytes (tables, rooms, profiles, Hold’em/PLO Rush and audit when present), including the in-memory default table definition when not yet persisted. Owner key is excluded; no raw cookies are stored in profile checkpoints. Source/backup symlinks are rejected, archives are private 0600 in a 0700 folder, writes use a temporary file then rename, and integrity validates manifest/allowed names/length/base64/SHA-256. Limit 20 MiB and ten archives; nothing auto-deletes and no restore endpoint overwrites running data. Console Create/View/Verify controls work in Chrome and native WKWebView. HTTP red test failed on missing route; final tests cover owner/origin/public-file guards, all six file kinds from active cash/both pools, exact original bytes, malformed/tampered archives, oversized archives/source, blocked storage paths, symlink refusal, limits and restart. An isolated backend extracted the snapshot and recovered identical active hand/token, login cookie and both Rush registrations. Owner table/audit regressions pass; native test creates/verifies through controls and still passes complete hand/payout/audio-graph/relaunch. Live update applied while idle: nine hosted snapshots and original-file hashes unchanged. Created/verified live baseline 58d3d22cc10add924218e4a8, 11121 bytes/four existing checkpoint files, under ~/.local/share/playgg-owner/backups/. This is independently specified local owner functionality, not observed native operator-admin parity. In-app restore, cross-device key recovery and broader moderation remain incomplete.

Chat/history refresh flicker follow-up: reproduced an existing chat message and reaction image being remounted when a later message arrived. network.mjs now builds a complete fresh chat list and applies the existing shared DOM updater once, retaining mounted reaction artwork; history/results/standings use the same retained-list update. Browser regression fails before the change on node identity and passes afterward. Full one-player/five-computer two-hand test passes independent split payout, 720-chip conservation, reveal/WIN/award ordering and reconnect. Sparse cash/late reservation regression passes through payout and backend restart. Node syntax check passes; live server serves the updated module (HTTP 200), available on next table reload without a backend restart. Native display-specific flicker and exact reference timing remain unverified.

Native flicker regression validation: tools/native-hand-step.js now retains actual mounted seat, portrait and already-dealt card references and asserts their identity on every visible WKWebView hand poll through showdown. Fresh tools/test-mac-app.sh run passes those checks together with local signup, owner backup/integrity, lobby/list/seat journey, five computers, independent Hold’em payout, 720 chips and exact hand/seat recovery after cold native relaunch. Captures regenerated at 1200×961. Decoded/rendered WebAudio peaks remain nonzero; no physical-speaker or native-reference timing claim. This is stronger renderer evidence for retained nodes, not a complete pixel/video flicker audit.

Wager-stack flicker: drawTable previously deleted every street wager before each state render. It now reconciles wagers by seat through the existing element updater, retaining unchanged chip images/labels and removing only wagers absent from the current street. Red short-handed browser assertion reproduced replacement of both posted blinds on an unrelated late reservation; final assertion passes while retaining betting draft/dialog/clock, then completes heads-up payout, next deal and server recovery. Full Hold’em and PLO five-computer tests each pass two hands, independent payouts, 720-chip conservation, reveal/WIN/transfer sequence, reconnect and release; additional assertion confirms no street wagers remain after settlement. Syntax passes and live server serves updated module HTTP 200; no backend restart or live game mutation. Native chip flight geometry/timing and display-specific flicker remain open.

Owner chat moderation: added owner-key/local-origin protected roster and mute/unmute endpoint, plus Manage chat controls for each hosted table. Opaque occupant subjects reject stale guest replacements; account mutes survive leave/rejoin in the same room, guest mutes apply to that seated identity. Text and reaction requests enforce the mute server-side; composer/reaction controls explain and reflect it while betting/seat access remain unaffected. Changes persist atomically with room state and append private owner audit entries, reporting audit failures separately; no public moderation operation or raw credentials. Red room test reproduced missing operation before implementation; final unit coverage includes validation, stale identity, idempotence, text/reaction/bypass rejection, unchanged hand, other-player chat, write rollback, restart and account rejoin/unmute. --moderation browser passes owner-key/foreign-origin/public-route guards, actual admin controls, composer disabling, retained betting input/open dialog, unchanged hand, real backend restart, other-browser message delivery after unmute and private audit; continues sparse hand/payout/reservation/restart regression. Existing owner API and full visible native WebKit login/host/seat/five-bot hand/payout/cold relaunch pass, including mounted seat/avatar/card checks. Live backend updated only after checking every hosted/Rush hand idle; all five original top-level file digests and nine public table snapshots unchanged. Live roster read verified, no live players muted. This is independently specified local owner functionality, not observed native private admin parity.

Readiness/payout interruption: a six-human browser regression reproduced Ready for next hand stripping the ongoing showdownTurn/showdownWin classes during the current award. drawTable now caches only the felt-relevant frame (capacity, hand number, perspective, names/settings, reserved stacks and hand), skips unchanged felt work, and still refreshes reactions/avatars. Ready and moderation-only changes update controls/status without cutting the current reveal, badge or pot motion; actual new hands and changed seats still render. Final hosted test waits through a remote poll after early Ready and confirms reveal/winner/award remain mounted, then passes three hand progression, independently verified unequal side pots/uncalled returns, folded privacy, chip conservation, server restart and replacement seating. Moderation/short-handed browser regression also passes. Native waiting-list evidence saved in home-art/rea/native-wait-list-strings.json with installed source digest and exact English eligibility copy. It establishes a same-condition playing-table prerequisite, but not the actual grouping/offer/expiry flow. Native getApp timed out; no native account action performed. Waiting-list behavior remains open rather than invented.

Measured Hold’em list spacing: retained native side of pair-select.png at 1200×957 has a 24px column header (raw y336–359), 32px rows (364–395, 400–431, 436–467) and 4px gaps; the local header/rows began 2px too low because its banner grid track was 142px rather than 140px, and rows were 31px. Added Hold’em-scoped CSS corrections (140px banner, 32px table rows) without changing other mode layouts. Red browser test reproduced 142px banner; final geometry asserts 140/32/36 banner/row/pitch in unscaled layout coordinates. Same-size local-selected-table.png regenerated at 1200×957 from live isolated six-seat data; comparison page confirms equally sized loaded native/local panes. Measurement source/digest/method in home-art/rea/holdem-list-geometry.json. Full one-player/five-bot two-hand test verifies payout, conserved chips, showdown sequence and reconnect. Avatar plates/badges, column widths, buy-in ranges and complete native controls remain visually different or unverified; this is a measured incremental match, not full parity.

Hold’em column geometry: measured native header boundaries from the retained capture at x20/196/402/512/628/680/732/784/830 (raw y342 divider samples). Added Hold’em-only catalog/list inset and proportional column tracks so all nine browser boundaries match within 1.1 physical pixels at 1200×957; native-height separator marks added to the header. The red browser test reproduced the old x17.06 first boundary. Regenerated current live-data local-selected-table.png; comparison viewer passes loaded equal-size frames. Source measurements and exact fractions recorded in holdem-list-geometry.json. Existing seat-block occupancy, selected-row preview/entry, full five-computer two-hand payout/reconnect verification remain covered. This matches the retained reference geometry; avatar plates/badges, buy-in ranges, original account area and full runtime parity remain unfinished.

Selected Hold’em preview portraits/plates: inspected the native 239×371 region of retained pair-select.png (x845,y218). It shows 50px portraits above separate roughly 60px name/stack plates, not the clone's 32px portraits inside a combined rounded box. Hold’em six-seat preview now uses 50px portraits, separate 60px dark plates, single-line ellipsized names with full-name titles, and the measured approximate six portrait centers (top/bottom/side rows). Existing local account avatars, names and chip stacks remain live. Native level badges, flags and blind markers are not fabricated. Red browser geometry test fails old preview; final Hold’em test asserts six 50px portraits outside their single-line plates, maintains identical preview avatar/table nodes across live stack changes, and completes two computer hands with independent payout, 720 chips and reconnect. PLO regression also passes two hands; its preview markup/geometry is retained. Updated 1200×957 local capture and comparison; source region/measurement scope in holdem-list-geometry.json. Native background texture, badges, flags and buy-in ranges remain different or unverified.

Cash buy-in ranges: owner console supports optional Minimum/Maximum buy-in for hosted Hold’em/PLO4/5/6; blank remains the existing fixed starting stack. Both integer limits must contain the default stack, with minimum at least four blinds and maximum 1,000,000; occupied limits are locked. Catalog table cells and selected felt show the configured range; seat form offers a constrained play-chip amount and preserves it across unchanged polls. Server validates it independently; new seats persist their selected buy-in, including reservations, with no current-hand chips added. Next cash deal funds that reservation; local bust rebuy uses that player's chosen buy-in. Existing funded balances continue carrying forward. No wallet or real-money balance is implied. Native reference establishes Buy-in Range presentation, not the seated amount-dialog geometry or operator min/max policy. Red room test accepted an invalid buy-in before implementation. Final unit covers invalid types/limits, no invalid reservation, 80/240 initial stacks, unchanged active hand on 100-chip reservation, restart/next-deal 420-chip total, fixed compatibility and all PLO card counts. --buy-in browser creates/edits limits through owner UI, rejects invalid owner limits and occupied edits, verifies actual catalog range, chooses unequal player amounts, cancels/rejoins a waiting seat, verifies independent payout and next-deal funding, and recovers after server restart. Existing owner, leave, short-handed, moderation and native full-hand/cold-relaunch regressions pass. Live backend activated only after idle audit; all five top-level checkpoint file hashes and nine public table views unchanged, no live table/seat mutation. Full native buy-in dialog/currency policy and account wallet remain unverified.

Selected-table position markers: public hosted summary now derives dealer/SB/BB from the actual hand's dealt seats and persisted big-blind position, excluding undealt reservations. Hold’em preview renders D with the existing local native-art disc and SB/BB blue labeled markers, kept separate from names/stacks; heads-up dealer/SB are offset to remain individually readable. No markers before a hand or for Short Deck's different ante/button rule. Red unit test reproduced missing positions; final room test covers six-seat rotation, sparse heads-up, late reservation, restart and no hole-card/token exposure. Full five-bot two-hand browser test verifies D/SB/BB at 0/1/2 then 1/2/3, three visible markers, no plate overlap, independent payout, 720 chips, persistent preview art and reconnect. Owner, buy-in, sparse and comparison tests pass. Updated same-size capture. Live backend activated after idle audit; all five file digests and nine public hand views unchanged. Labels and approximate placement follow retained native preview; exact blue-marker artwork/timing, rank badges and flags remain unverified.

Custom profile images: native My Game Profile capture shows Upload Image; local control is now functional. PNG/JPEG/WebP files up to 5 MiB are center-cropped/resized to 256×256 in-browser, previewed without persistence, and saved only on Apply. Close/Escape discards the draft; stale decode results cannot reopen it. All/Custom categories and one saved custom choice support selecting a standard avatar and returning to the saved custom image. Server requires the signed-in local session and exact origin; validates canonical bounded PNG, chunk checksums, square RGBA/RGB dimensions, decompression size and filter bytes, then strips all ancillary metadata/animation chunks. Content-hashed local PNG URLs keep image bytes out of polling state; avatars follow account into live seat, public preview and spectator with unchanged betting controls. No dependencies or external uploads. Native WebKit initially exposed extra eXIf metadata; normalization resolves it, with explicit stripping regression. ### Source-derived bet rounding

REA exported selected BettingInfo, BettingUI and OptionBettingPage classes from the installed client. Native normal shortcut amounts ceiling upward to minChipUnit when Don’t round to nearest blind is checked, otherwise to small blind (button-blind tables use ceil(buttonBlind/20)*10); legal raise-to bounds clamp afterward. Native opening button explicitly bypasses this rounding; Pot/Max use separate paths. Local normal shortcuts previously used nearest-integer rounding and the checkbox was disabled. Enabled the preference in the existing draft/Apply/Cancel/storage flow, replaced normal percentage calculation with source-derived ceiling, and retained existing preset choices for legacy stored objects. Local practice minChipUnit=1; real-wallet currency behavior is not claimed.

Fixed local sweep amounts to collect only the pending street contribution, excluding uncalled returns and previously collected streets. The four-browser 30/70/100/4 fixture gathers 30/70/70/4=174. A six-seat multi-street hand verifies that sweep totals match every final contribution exactly once; two hands preserve 720 chips and reconnect. AoF return composition follows native newest-increment-first subtraction: [2,30] minus12 becomes [2,18], rather than a collapsed [20] pile. Unit tests also cover a return crossing the increment boundary. Evidence: `home-art/rea/uncalled-collection.json`. Native staged all-in presentation and per-side-pot award choreography remain open.

## Per-pot results and folded-layer tie correction
Folded contribution boundaries with identical eligible seats now form one pot before odd-chip allocation. For [10,10,10,3,1], with the last two folded and the first two tied, the single34-chip pot pays17/17, correcting18/16. Each settled pot now retains its amount, eligible seats and payout vector. New hand results persist and display the main/side-pot breakdown; older saved results remain readable.

Four browsers verify main94/side80 and return30 separately, independent payouts, rendered labels, folded privacy, BB display and a post-settlement server restart. Variant and backup regressions pass. The live server was updated while idle with all persistent file hashes unchanged. Evidence: `home-art/rea/per-pot-results.json`. Native per-pot award animations and staged all-in presentation remain open.

## Separate per-pot award animations
The table creates one award for each pot/winner share, starting from recovered TableSetting pot positions (main850/663; first side692/706; remaining indexed positions retained). AoF native glyph and stop-path composition now accepts each origin. Each share credits the displayed balance once; repeated animation completion/cancel cannot double-credit. Older results without per-pot details retain their previous aggregate fallback.

Four-browser tests verify separate94/80 award nodes, source-origin geometry, a shared winner's accumulated balance, duplicate-event protection, payout/privacy/reload/server restart and next hand. AoF single-pot native path and128-chip conservation regressions pass. Evidence: `home-art/rea/per-pot-awards.json`. Static multi-pot piles remain aggregated; normal-table moving art/path and multi-pot AoF geometry need further native/rendered verification. All-in staging and native audio scheduling remain open.

## Separate static multi-pot piles at settlement
Multi-pot settlement now shows distinct native GPD piles and amount labels at recovered indexed TableSetting positions, suppressing the aggregate collected graphic. Existing chip denomination/group/cap logic and original table0049 label background are reused. Piles release as payout starts and do not replay after settled reload.

Four browsers verify main94/side80 labels, exact decoded36×35 glyphs, glyph sums and source geometry, including a split40/40 side-pot outcome. Chat preserves the same pile nodes and animation objects. Per-pot credits, removal, privacy, BB display, server restart and next hand pass; AoF single-pot regression passes. ## Unequal-stack AoF award-path verification
Added `node network-browser-test.mjs --aof-sidepots`. An isolated server fixture starts with stacks48/32/20/48; four separate browser storage contexts perform Fold/All in through real table controls. Independent hand ranks verify main60/side24 and a separate16-chip return. Actual CSS animations are paused/sampled/restored at0/400/1600/1999ms: each path starts at its indexed native pot origin, stops140×playerScale before the correct cell, holds, and finishes at that cell. Native moving pile amounts match each pot award.

The test also verifies folded-card privacy, no WIN for returns alone, displayed final balances,148-chip conservation and server restart without replay. Measured paths and source hashes are saved in `home-art/rea/aof-sidepot-paths.json`. This is presentation/recovery coverage with seeded starting stacks; it does not establish native AoF buy-in/stack-cap parity or native runtime timing. The staged all-in hole/board sequence remains open.

## Native AoF legal-action contract
REA confirms that AofMainActionUI chooses Call/callAmount when available, then overrides with Raise/maxRaiseToAmount when Raise is available. TableMediator supplies the values from TablePlayer; the maximum is committedAmount+callAmount+maxBetAmount. BettingInfo treats full-stack all-in as enabled only when the supplied maximum covers balance+committedAmount. The inspected client classes consume server limits; they do not establish a universal AoF stack cap.

Added engine regressions for both cases: a late48-stack player calls only28 after others commit20/32, keeping16 uncommitted; an earlier48-stack raiser can commit48 and later receive16 back. Each case conserves148. Separate native AutoBuyIn/per-table AutoRemoveChips options were identified but their defaults, cap and local transfer behavior remain unverified/incomplete. Evidence: `home-art/rea/aof-action-contract.json`. No guessed cap was added.

## Payout completion gates the next hand
The local Ready control is disabled while any award animation is pending, and its operation handler enforces the same gate. The last completed/cancelled award refreshes controls without replaying the table scene. This prevents an early manual next-hand request from cutting off showdown/payout. Explicit Leave remains available; null-safe animation callbacks tolerate the cleared session.

AoF side-pot browsers verify disabled Ready, no submitted ready state and automatic unlock. Six hosted sessions verify the ignored early click preserves reveal/WIN/award, followed by a second unequal-stack hand, restart and replacement. Five-computer two-hand regression passes. An explicit Leave during payout verifies no page errors, preserved payout/returns history, cleared departing waiting seat and exact cash-out receipt. Evidence: `home-art/rea/payout-action-gate.json`.

REA also confirms reveal order is built from incoming OnShowSelectedCards notifications; OnStartCardRun disables action, and OnCommunityCards supplies separate board stages. Original server notification order/inter-message timing is not established by local seat order. Full staged all-in presentation remains open.

## Win-pot sound follows the payout stage
Recovered MotionView3D.potMoveToWinners plays SOUND_WIN_POT once before iterating a pot's winner vector. The local animated settlement now defers the semantic WinPot cue until the first award animation for that pot starts, deduplicating multiple winners. New hands reset the set; closed/changed sessions cannot emit an old award cue. Reduced-motion handling retains the existing immediate semantic cue path. Initial/reconnected snapshots still produce no semantic win cue.

`node network-browser-test.mjs --aof-sidepots --payout-audio` uses a controlled royal-flush board with five winner shares across two pots. A decoded native-buffer fingerprint identifies actual WebAudio starts: exactly two, both after payout motion begins. No animation scrubbing occurs in this audio test. Independent payout,148-chip conservation and restart checks pass. Standard indexed-path and five-computer two-hand/720-chip regressions, mute/volume/pending-resume tests pass. Evidence and native MP3 hash: `home-art/rea/payout-audio-timing.json`. Physical speakers, native runtime sound levels/phase and full all-in staging remain unverified.

## Staged local all-in presentation
Fresh contested all-in runouts now have a separate, non-mutating presentation plan. Default source budgets are used:400ms collection, selected-card reveals spanning2s,200ms board throws and300ms flop spread. Reveals follow eligible entries in the local public card array; the original server notification order remains unverified. Existing own cards stay visible until their reveal starts. Newly dealt board cards remain hidden until their stage, with separate flop/turn/river cues. WIN, pot release and payout are offset until the board stages finish. The moving throw itself is not yet drawn.

The server still settles atomically and retains authority over cards, payouts, returns and chip balances. The plan is transient: reload/catch-up skips it, a new hand clears it, and Leave safely clears the presentation. Ready stays disabled until awards finish. Reduced-motion snapshots use the existing immediate path.

Actual unscripted CSS event traces verify hole flip → flop/turn/river → WIN → payout, with no immediate board exposure. A tied fixture verifies five shares but two decoded native pot-start sounds. Mid-runout reload verifies the final board, unchanged payouts, no replay and Ready availability. AoF pose/path, six-session unequal second hand/restart/replacement and five-bot two-hand720-chip regressions pass. Pure plan tests cover all starting streets, immutable snapshots and skip conditions. Evidence: `home-art/rea/showdown-sequence.json`; test: `node showdown-plan-test.mjs`. Exact native notification order, runtime speed, incoming throw trajectory and full pixel/audio parity remain open.

## Reduced-motion changes during showdown
Fixed a live preference-change bug: a staged board could retain delayed presentation after reduced motion was enabled. The media-query listener now clears the transient plan, cancels/finalizes pending awards idempotently, removes obsolete sweeps and redraws authoritative final cards/balances. Ready becomes available; restoring motion does not replay the hand. Remaining pot cues are preserved without repeating cues already issued, through the existing mute/volume engine. CSS overrides also suppress card/back/board/WIN motion; queued WIN image changes check the current preference.

Headless tests pass with reduced motion at launch, switched on during card runout, and switched on during payout. They verify visible board, final balances, no page errors, Ready availability, unchanged server result and no replay on restoring motion. Normal staged audio/unequal-stack/restart regression passes. Evidence: `home-art/rea/reduced-motion-runout.json`. The native macOS toggle and dedicated Omaha integration remain untested; foreground QA remains disabled.

Normal sequences pass for all three formats, including3×hole-count reveal events before the board, then WIN/payout. Reduced motion from launch passes for PLO4; a mid-runout switch passes for PLO6. Every tested final card stays within the table, tested rank/suit regions avoid the WIN text bounds, balances conserve50, folded cards remain private and restart skips replay. Evidence matrix: `home-art/rea/omaha-runout-verification.json`; per-case results: `plo*-runout*.json` in the same directory. These are isolated fixtures, not a claim of native same-state visual parity or the full owner/account journey.

## REA Helmet contract — documented, not enabled locally

- Native social helmet is visible for the own player's isMuteMode and opens HELMET_TAKE_OFF_POPUP. Activation has a separate HELMET_PUT_ON_POPUP; both are340x265 with140x120 this/all choices. enabled=false means selected in TableHelmetModeButton; activation resets selection to this table.
- Confirm emits CHAT_MUTE_STATE_UPDATE{isPutOnHelmet,isAll}; popup mediator→UPDATE_CHAT_MUTE_STATUS. Activation Cancel emits a refresh event; deactivation Cancel only closes. These paths differ from the chat-option row's direct CHAT_MUTE_UPDATE toggle.
- Native request3145 carries IsChatMute and optional RushSessionId. Its request code resets the global preference on unmute and leaves active audioSpace on mute. Response handler does not itself update the player; callback4079 applies isMuteMode, closes SnapCam, hides own current emoticon on mute, refreshes menu and changes the player's helmet.
- ChatBubbleView filters new ChatBox additions when muted; its setter does not clear existing children. This does not establish server delivery suppression. Global commands address all open tables and separately maintain putOnHelmetMode; future-table/restart semantics remain unproven.
- Source TablePopupAssets333/334,336..341,57,342/343,443/444 bind the dialog/chat artwork. The player helmet uses Starling player_cell71 at0.8scale with0.2s tween; desktop duplicate is not substituted. Native runtime/chrome/frame/speaker comparison remains open.

Native constant labels are not counted as complete functional parity.47menu branches remain documented separately. Ordinary Holdem/PLO4/5/6/Rush HUDs count steals from unopened CO/BTN/SB positions and re-raises after exactly one opening raise, using legal opportunities as local denominators. ### Continuation-bet HUD — recorded local samples

- Flop/Turn CB, FCB, CCB, RCB now derive from completed recorded action traces in ordinary rooms and Rush; table/pool scope is explicit, missing history stays unknown.
- Native per-street color thresholds retained from `HudAverageView.as`; no panel geometry changes. See `docs/rea/continuation-bet-hud.md`.
- Engine and six-player persisted-ledger tests plus muted headless browser assertions at850/1196px cover actual hand/payout, own CB/response percentages, native color thresholds and cold restart. Release requires exact-head installed QA.

### Primary betting controls — keyboard order and source audit

Seven native action-panel classes establish the source button textures, geometry, pressed label offsets and hotkey identities. Ordinary local controls now follow their visible left-to-right DOM/Tab order. Isolated browser checks cover focus without a wager, hover/press/cancel, source geometry, full-hand payout and restart. Native physical seated/Tab parity is not claimed; see `docs/rea/main-action-controls.md`.

### Owner monitor and eliminated tournament previews

The owner can monitor authoritative chips, commitments,actor,phase,levels,eliminations,finishing places and settled prizes.
